&-m_{1+4}^{2}\chi_{2,3}\chi_{4,1}\cdot x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\big[x_{1}x_{4}-x_{4}^{2}+2x_{3}^{2}+2x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{4}+x_{2}x_{4}\big]\\
&+m_{3}m_{1+4}\chi_{2,3}\chi_{4,1}\cdot 2x_{2}^{2}x_{4}(x_{1}x_{4}-x_{4}^{2}+2x_{3}^{2}+2x_{2}^{2}).\end{split}

(9.57)

Using (
9.17
) and (
9.23
) we can rewrite the formula (
9.53
) as

\begin{split}&4J_{1,4}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=m_{4}^{2}\cdot 8x_{2}x_{4}^{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+m_{3}^{2}\cdot 8x_{2}x_{3}^{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\\
&-m_{3}^{2}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 4x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})^{2}-m_{1+2}^{2}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 4x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})^{2}+m_{3}m_{1+2}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 8x_{2}x_{4}^{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\\
&+m_{4}^{2}\chi_{2,1}\cdot 2x_{2}x_{4}\big[x_{4}(x_{1}x_{2}+x_{2}^{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2})+x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\big]\\
&+m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2,1}\cdot 2x_{2}x_{3}\big[x_{3}(x_{1}x_{2}+x_{2}^{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2})+x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\big]\\
&-m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2,1}\chi_{4,3}\cdot x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\big[x_{1}x_{2}+x_{2}^{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2}+x_{2}x_{3}+x_{2}x_{4}\big]\\
&-m_{1+2}^{2}\chi_{2,1}\chi_{4,3}\cdot x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\big[x_{1}x_{2}+x_{2}^{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2}+x_{2}x_{3}-x_{2}x_{4}\big]\\
&+m_{3}m_{1+2}\chi_{2,1}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 2x_{2}x_{4}^{2}(x_{1}x_{2}+x_{2}^{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2}).\end{split}

(9.58)

Finally, using (
9.18
) we can rewrite the formula (
9.54
) as

4J_{1,5}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4}):=-m_{2}^{2}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{2}^{2}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-m_{4}^{2}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{4}^{2}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}).

(9.59)

Contributions of
K_{2}^{1}
. Using (
9.2
)–(
9.4
) we calculate the components

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=-m_{1}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|(2-\chi_{2+4}),\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{2},y_{1},y_{3},y_{4})=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{3},y_{2},y_{1},y_{4})=m_{3}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}||y_{2}|^{1/2}\chi_{2+4},\end{split}

\begin{split}&K_{2}^{1}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{4},y_{3})=-2m_{1}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|-m_{1}^{2}\chi_{2+4}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}+y_{4}|\\
&+m_{1}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{4}|\chi_{4,3}(2-\chi_{2,1})+m_{1}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{2}|\chi_{2,3}(2-\chi_{4,1}),\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{2},y_{1},y_{4},y_{3})=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{4},y_{2},y_{1},y_{3})=0.\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{1},y_{3},y_{4},y_{2})=-m_{1}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|(2-\chi_{2+4}),\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{3},y_{1},y_{4},y_{2})=m_{3}^{2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}||y_{4}|^{1/2}\chi_{2+4},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{4},y_{1},y_{3},y_{2})=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{2},y_{3},y_{4},y_{1})=-2m_{2}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{3/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(&y_{3},y_{2},y_{4},y_{1})=-2m_{3}^{2}|y_{3}|^{3/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}-m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2+4}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}+y_{4}|\\
&+2m_{3}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{2}|\chi_{2,3}+2m_{3}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{4}|\chi_{4,3}\\
&-m_{3}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{1}+y_{4}|\chi_{4,1}\chi_{2,3}-m_{3}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{1}+y_{2}|\chi_{2,1}\chi_{4,3}\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{1}(y_{4},y_{2},y_{3},y_{1})=-2m_{4}^{2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{3/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2},\end{split}

Therefore, using (
5.6
),

4SK_{2}^{1}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=\sum_{l\in\{1,\ldots,4\}}4H_{2,l}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4}),

(9.60)

where

\begin{split}4H_{2,1}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},&y_{4})=-m_{1}^{2}\cdot 8(x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{2}-x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{2}+x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}x_{3}^{2})\\
&+m_{1}^{2}\chi_{2+4}\cdot 4[x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{2}-x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{2}-x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})]\\
&+m_{1}^{2}\chi_{2,3}\cdot 8x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})-m_{1}^{2}\chi_{2,3}\chi_{4,1}\cdot 4x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\\
&+m_{1}^{2}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 8x_{1}x_{4}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-m_{1}^{2}\chi_{4,3}\chi_{2,1}\cdot 4x_{1}x_{4}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

(9.61)

\begin{split}4H_{2,2}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},&y_{4})=m_{2}^{2}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{3},\end{split}

(9.62)

\begin{split}4H_{2,3}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},&y_{4})=m_{3}^{2}\cdot 8x_{3}^{3}x_{2}x_{4}\\
&+m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2+4}\cdot 4\big[x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{2}+x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{2}\big]\\
&-m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2,3}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+m_{3}^{2}\chi_{4,1}\chi_{2,3}\cdot 4x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\\
&-m_{3}^{2}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+m_{3}^{2}\chi_{2,1}\chi_{4,3}\cdot 4x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

(9.63)

\begin{split}4H_{2,4}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},&y_{4})=m_{4}^{2}\cdot 8x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{3}.\end{split}

(9.64)

Contributions of
K_{2}^{2}
.
From the formula (
4.101
), and using the definition (
4.74
),
it is easy to see that there are no nontrivial components. Thus

4SK_{2}^{2}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=0.

(9.65)

Contributions of
K_{2}^{3}
. Using the formulas (
4.102
) and (
9.5
)–(
9.7
) we calculate

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=+6A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{4},y_{3}+y_{2})\cdot|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{2}|^{3/2}(2-\chi_{2,3}),\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{2},y_{1},y_{3},y_{4})=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{3},y_{2},y_{1},y_{4})=&-6A^{\prime}(y_{3},y_{4},y_{1}+y_{2})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|\chi_{2,1}\\
&-3A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}||y_{2}|\chi_{4,3},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{4},y_{3})=+12A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{3},y_{2}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}||y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}+y_{4}|^{3/2},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{2},y_{1},y_{4},y_{3})=&-6A^{\prime}(y_{3},y_{2},y_{1}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|\chi_{4,1}\\
&+3A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{4},y_{3}+y_{2})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|(2|y_{3}|+|y_{2}|)\chi_{2,3},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{4},y_{2},y_{1},y_{3})=&-6A^{\prime}(y_{3},y_{4},y_{1}+y_{2})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|\chi_{2,1}\\
&+3A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|(2|y_{3}|+|y_{4}|)\chi_{4,3},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{1},y_{3},y_{4},y_{2})=+6A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{3}+y_{4}|^{3/2}(2-\chi_{4,3}),\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{3},y_{1},y_{4},y_{2})=&-6A^{\prime}(y_{3},y_{2},y_{1}+y_{4})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{4}|^{1/2}|y_{1}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|\chi_{4,1}\\
&-3A^{\prime}(y_{1},y_{4},y_{3}+y_{2})\cdot|y_{3}+y_{2}|^{1/2}|y_{3}|^{1/2}|y_{2}||y_{4}|\chi_{2,3},\end{split}

\begin{split}K_{2}^{3}(y_{4},y_{1},y_{3},y_{2})=0,\end{split}

\begin{split}&K_{2}^{3}(y_{2},y_{3},y_{4},y_{1})=K_{2}^{3}(y_{3},y_{2},y_{4},y_{1})=K_{2}^{3}(y_{4},y_{2},y_{3},y_{1})=0.\end{split}

Therefore, using (
5.6
),

4SK_{2}^{3}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})=\sum_{l\in\{1,2,3,4,6\}}4I_{2,l}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})

(9.66)

where

(9.89)

9.2.
Proof of Proposition
6.2

We restrict now to the case of resonant frequencies and prove the identity (
6.30
). In terms of the variables
x_{j}
the two main conditions
y_{1}+y_{2}+y_{3}+y_{4}=0
and
|y_{1}|^{1/2}+|y_{2}|^{1/2}-|y_{3}|^{1/2}-|y_{4}|^{1/2}=0
show that

x_{1}=x_{3}+x_{4}-x_{2}\,\,\text{ and }\,\,x_{2}x_{3}+x_{2}x_{4}-x_{3}x_{4}=0.

(9.90)

We now re-arrange the formulas in §
9.1
above to calculate

\begin{split}\sum_{a\in\{1,\ldots,9\}}&4SK_{1}^{a}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})-\sum_{a\in\{1,\ldots,4\}}4SK_{2}^{a}(y_{1},y_{2},y_{3},y_{4})\\
&=X_{1}m_{1}^{2}+X_{2}m_{2}^{2}+X_{3}m_{3}^{2}+X_{4}m_{4}^{2}+X_{5}m_{1+2}^{2}+X_{6}m_{1+4}^{2}+X_{7}m_{1+3}^{2}\\
&\quad\,+Y_{1}m_{1}m_{3+2}+Y_{2}m_{1}m_{3+4}+Y_{3}m_{3}m_{1+2}+Y_{4}m_{3}m_{1+4}+Y_{5}m_{1}m_{3}.\end{split}

(9.91)

Notice how we are organizing the terms according to the factors
m_{1},m_{2}
and so on.

\chi
, see for example (

).

The coefficient

.

&+\chi_{2,3}\cdot X_{1}^{6}+\chi_{4,1}\cdot X_{1}^{7}+\chi_{2,3}\chi_{4,1}\cdot X_{1}^{8},\end{split}

(9.92)

where

\begin{split}X_{1}^{1}&=8(x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{2}-x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{2}+x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}x_{3}^{2})-8x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\\
&+8x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+8x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{2}&=4x_{1}^{2}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{4}-x_{2})+8x_{1}x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{2}-4[x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}^{2}-x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}^{2}-x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})]\\
&-4x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-4x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{3}&=4x_{1}x_{3}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-4x_{3}x_{4}x_{1}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{4}&=-2x_{1}x_{4}\big[-x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})\big]-8x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\\
&+2x_{1}x_{4}[2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+x_{2}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})],\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{5}&=-2x_{1}^{2}x_{4}^{2}x_{2}-2x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{2}x_{1}+x_{1}x_{4}\big[-x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})\big]\\
&+x_{1}x_{4}\big[x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})\big]+4x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\\
&-x_{1}x_{4}[2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})+x_{2}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})]\\
&-x_{4}x_{1}\big[x_{2}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}-x_{3}x_{4})-2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})\big],\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{6}&=2x_{1}x_{2}\big[x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})\big]-8x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\\
&+2x_{1}x_{2}[-2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+x_{4}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})],\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{7}&=-4x_{1}x_{3}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+4x_{3}x_{2}x_{1}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}),\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{1}^{8}&=2x_{1}^{2}x_{2}^{2}x_{4}-2x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{2}x_{1}-x_{1}x_{2}\big[x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})\big]\\
&+x_{1}x_{2}\big[x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+x_{1}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})\big]+4x_{1}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\\
&-x_{1}x_{2}[-2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})+x_{4}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})]\\
&-x_{2}x_{1}\big[x_{4}(2x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{2})+2x_{3}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\big].\end{split}

Clearly,
X_{1}^{3}=X_{1}^{7}=0
. Also, using the identities (
9.90
) it is easy to see that
X_{1}^{1}=X_{1}^{2}=X_{1}^{4}=X_{1}^{5}=X_{1}^{6}=X_{1}^{8}=0
; for example

\begin{split}X_{1}^{1}&=8x_{1}(x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})(x_{2}x_{4}+x_{2}x_{3}-x_{3}x_{4})=0.\end{split}

Therefore
X_{1}=0
as desired.

\begin{split}X_{3}^{4}&=4x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})^{2}-4x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})^{2}+8x_{3}x_{4}x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})-8x_{2}x_{3}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2}),\end{split}

\begin{split}X_{3}^{5}&=2x_{3}^{2}x_{2}^{2}x_{4}+2x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{2}x_{3}+x_{2}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{4}^{2})(x_{1}x_{2}+x_{3}x_{2}-2x_{3}^{2}-2x_{4}^{2}+x_{2}^{2}+x_{2}x_{4})\\

&-x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\big[x_{1}x_{4}-x_{4}^{2}+2x_{3}^{2}+2x_{2}^{2}+x_{3}x_{4}-x_{2}x_{4}\big]\\
&-4x_{3}x_{2}x_{4}(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})-2x_{1}x_{4}(x_{3}-x_{2})(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\\
&+2x_{4}\big[x_{3}x_{2}(x_{1}x_{4}-x_{4}^{2}+2x_{3}^{2}+2x_{2}^{2})+x_{1}(x_{3}-x_{2})(x_{3}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})\big].\end{split}

9.37
), (
9.34
),

\begin{split}Y_{5}&=-8x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{2}(x_{1}-x_{3})\chi_{2+4}+8\chi_{2+4}(x_{2}^{2}x_{4}^{3}-x_{4}^{2}x_{2}^{3})=0.\end{split}

Therefore we verified that
X_{1}=\ldots=X_{7}=Y_{1}=\ldots=Y_{5}=0
, and the conclusion of Proposition
6.2
follows from (
9.91
).
